: searching    ( key -- )
 local oldline                          ( last line we were on, to clean up )
 eos prevchar inwindow                  ( find line containing cursor )
 if oldline to else oldline off then    ( and save it, if it's in the window )
 stripshifts dup undo =                 ( if the key was the undo key )
 newlex pattern @ -2 = or and           ( and there isn't any pattern yet )
 checkspell@  and                       ( and we have a spellcheck ROM )
 if  drop  spellcheckleap exit  then    ( spelling checker )
 dup erase = newlex and  if drop exit then              ( noop )
 newlex pattern @ -1 = or               ( if newlex or nonchar in pattern )
 if newlex off 0 patlen to then         ( omit old pattern )
 unexpand start-search dup erase =      ( remove char from pat, try over )
 if drop pattdel beot eos to  eos prevchar bos to
 else pattadd clear-auto patlen 0=      ( reset start pt wtih first char )
   if beot eos to eos prevchar bos to then
   lexxing not if exit then             ( if not lexxing anymore, we're done! )
 then   pattern patlen direction buildtable
 direction  ( on=search right )
 if beot patlen 1- advanceptr           ( skip patlen after beot )
   eor prevchar search>                 ( through end of text )
   dup 0= learnbuff 0= and              ( if not there and not learning )
   if drop bor patlen 1- advanceptr  gap prevchar search> ( begin upto sel)
     ?dup 0=                            ( if not there )
      if beot dup patlen 1- advanceptr eor prevchar  min search> then
   then
 else ( off=search to the left )
   bor gap prevchar prevchar search<    ( see if any before selection)
   dup 0= learnbuff 0= and              ( if not there )
   if drop beot eor prevchar search<    ( then check text after the gap)
   then
 then
 dup 0= patlen 2 > and          ( if didn't find it, & patlen is three or more )
 if pattern c@ pattern          ( see if pattern is all the same )
   begin nextchar  dup pattern patlen + <
   while  2dup c@ <>
   until
   pattern patlen + <           ( if not all the same )
   if drop                      ( just clean up )
   else drop lexerr error       ( if same, give error on usage )
   then
 then oldline update!  end-search ;     ( and finish off the search )

: do-lex       ( key -- )
   lexxing not if init-lex then         ( first lex event )
   ?lex ?rex or ?ctl or not if drop finish-lex exit then        ( see if done )
   %lex curop to                        ( so that we will beep each time )
   ?lex ?rex and ?split 0= and if drop expand exit then ( make selection )
   ?rex direction to                    ( set up direction for coming search )
   dup shiftkey? 0=                     ( test for non-shift key )
   if searching                         ( if not, add to pattern and search )
   else upkey? not ?ctl and             ( repeat search? )
      if newlex                         ( first search attempt during lex )
           if newlex off then           ( so that we know this wasn't a tap )
           pattern @ -1 =               ( see if pattern was non-character )
           if lex-scroll                ( scroll away )
               auto 0= if set-auto then ( and make sure we will repeat )
               exit                     ( and done now )
         then pattern @ -2 = checkspell@ and    ( were we checking spelling? )
         if indspell rule               ( say what we are doing )
            spellcheckagain             ( and off to check the spelling! )
            ?kval upkey? ?panic or      ( if an upkey or we panicked )
            if clear-auto               ( make sure we won't auto-repeat )
            else auto 0= if set-auto then       ( and make sure we will repeat )
            then exit
         then
         unexpand start-search          ( collapse and start searching )
         ?kval upkey?                   ( if the key is an upkey )
         if clear-auto                  ( then don't set the auto-repeat )
         else auto 0= if set-auto  then         ( else set it, if needed )
         then
         pattern patlen direction buildtable
         research                       ( and do the search )
   then  then ;

code <sortmap> nx ) jsr, ;c
   tc' <sortmap> 2+ tromaddr' sortmap !         ( initialize the variable )

  ( 0 means the character doesn't participate in the string comparison,
   -1 means the character is always checked for international special case,
   -2 means the character is check only if intlspecial is set )

;s      INTERNATIONAL SORT ORDER               VO1   7/30/87

Upper and lower case letters are equal in order

Ordered from lowest to highest:

Space (20) = Perm Space (93) = Overstrike Space (AA)

code comparestrings  ( $1 $2 -- ad1 ad2 compareflag: -1=$1<$2, 0=$1=$2, 1=$1>$2
   a0 = temp            d1 = limit of $1
   a1 = address of $1   d2 = limit of $2
   a2 = address of $2   d3 = char from $1
   a5 = sortmap         d4 = char from $2
                        d5 = digitflag for $1
                        d6 = digitflag for $2
                        d7 = compareflag )
 sp )+ d2 move,  sp )+ a0 move, ( address and limit of $2 )
 sp )+ d1 move,  sp )+ a1 move, ( address and limit of $1 )
 (regs a2 a5 d4 d5 d6 d7 to) sp -) movem,
 a0 a2 move,                                    ( shuffle information )
 0 #n d7 moveq,  d7 d3 move,  d7 d4 move,       ( clear flag & char holders )
 i' sortmap a5 move,
 begin,  a1 a0 move,  3 bsr,
   d4 d3 move,  d6 d5 move,             ( d3 = xlated char1, d5 = digitflag1 )
   a2 a0 move,  3 bsr,                  ( d4 = xlated char2, d6 = digitflag2 )
   d5 .w tst,  pl                       ( char1 = digit? )
   if,  d6 .w tst,  2 pl bra,           ( yes, char2 = digit? yes, goto 2 )
      1 :l  d3 d4 .b cmp,  cs           ( no, compare char1 and char2 )
      if,  1 #n d7 moveq,               ( comparflag, char1 > char2 )
      else,  -1 #n d7 moveq,            ( comparflag, char1 < char2 )
      then,  2 bra,                     ( all done )
   else,  d6 .w tst,  1 pl bra,  then,  ( both are now non-digits )
   d3 d4 .b cmp,  1 ne bra,             ( both are now equal )
   a1 a0 move,  tc' ^nextchar jsr,  a0 a1 move,
   a2 a0 move,  tc' ^nextchar jsr,  a0 a2 move,
   a1 d1 cmp,  2 ge bra,
   a2 d2 cmp,  lt
 until,  2 :l  d7 d0 move,  a2 a0 move, ( all done, protect information )
 (regs a2 a5 d4 d5 d6 d7 from) sp )+ movem,
 a1 sp -) move,  a0 sp -) move,  d0 sp -) move,
 next,                  ( assume equal.  $< will test for no more $ )
 3 :l  -1 #n d6 moveq,  d6 d0 move,             ( digitflag=0, specialflag=-1 )
 begin,  a0 ) .b d4 move,
   a5 d4 0 xw)d d4 .b move,  eq                 ( d4 = translated char )
 while,  1 #n a0 addq,  again,
 -1 #n d4 .b cmp,  6 eq bra,                    ( is this tilde or "enyeah"? )
 -2 #n d4 .b cmp,  4 eq bra,
 3a #n d4 .b cmp,  cs                           ( d6 = digitflag )
 if,  2f #n d4 .b cmp,  nc
   if,  0 #n d6 moveq,  then,  then,  rts,
 4 :l  a0 1 )d d0 .b move,                      ( char must not be accented )
 &firsthid #n d0 .b and,                        ( d0 never = &skip )
 &skip #n d0 .b cmp,  eq                        ( attribute byte? )
 if,  a0 2 )d d0 .b move,  &firsthid #n d0 .b and,  then,       ( yes, getnext )
 &firstacc #n d0 .b cmp,  5 eq bra,             ( accented -> sort normally )
 i' intlspecial tst,  eq
 if,  5 :l  0 #n d0 moveq,  then,               ( use normal sort order )
 6 :l  a0 ) d4 .b move,                         ( maybe special sort order? )
 i' intlsortmap a5 move,                        ( special international cases )
 begin,  a5 ) d4 .b cmp,  ne                    ( there are 7 special cases )
 while,  5 #n a5 addq,  again,
 d0 tst,  8 eq bra,
 a5 1 )d d0 .b move,
 a0 1 )d d0 .b cmp,  7 eq bra,                  ( 1st = nextchar/accent )
 a0 2 )d d0 .b cmp,  7 eq bra,                  ( "" )
 a5 2 )d d0 .b move,
 a0 1 )d d0 .b cmp,  7 eq bra,                  ( 2nd = nextchar/accent )
 a0 2 )d d0 .b cmp,  eq                         ( "" )
 if,  7 :l  a5 3 )d d4 .b move,                 ( special char order )
 else,  8 :l  a5 4 )d d4 .b move,               ( normal char order )
 then,  i' sortmap a5 move,  rts, ;c            ( restore sortmap )

code signed?  ( $1 $2 -- flag )
 ( true = at least one is a signed number, the other is at least a number )

 ( a0 = address within string   d1 = limit of string
   a1 = table addresses         d2 = character
                                d3 = digitflag )

 0 #n d2 moveq,                                 ( clear char )
 sp )+ d1 move,  sp )+ a0 move,  2 bsr,         ( check sign of $2 )
 sp )+ d1 move,  sp ) a0 move,  d3 sp ) move,
 2 bsr,  d3 tst,  ne                            ( check sign of $1 )
 if,  sp ) tst,  1 mi bra,  ne
   if,  d3 tst,  mi
      if,  1 :l  -1 #n sp ) move,  next,
 then,  then,  then,  0 #n sp ) move,  next,
2 :l  i' sortmap a1 move,  0 #n d0 moveq,
   begin,  a0 ) d0 .b move,     ( examine sign )
   a1 d0 0 xw)d d2 .b move,  eq                 ( d2 = translated char )
 while,  1 #n a0 addq,  again,
 ascii + #n d0 .b cmpi,  3 eq .b bra,
 ascii - #n d0 .b cmpi,  ne
 if,  4 bsr,  1 #n d3 andi,  rts,               ( d3 = digitflag )
 else,  3 :l                                    ( first char = sign )
   tc' ^nextchar jsr,  d1 a0 cmp,  lt
   if,  begin,  a0 ) d0 .b move,                ( not past limit )
         a1 d0 0 xw)d d2 .b move,  eq           ( d2 = translated char )
      while,  1 #n a0 addq,
      again,
      4 :l  a0 ) d0 .b move,                    ( set flag )
      i' dpoint 3 + d0 .b cmp,  5 eq bra,       ( d0 = char )
      i' commapun 3 + d0 .b cmp,  5 eq bra,
      t' #table +ttable a1 move,  2 #n a1 addq,
      i' #chars d3 move,  1 #n d3 subq,
      begin,  a1 )+ d0 .b cmp,  d3 eq -until,  eq
      if,  5 :l  -1 #n d3 moveq,                ( char = digit, comma or dp )
      else,  0 #n d3 moveq,                     ( d3 = digitflag )
 then,  then,  then,  rts,  ;c

code checksigns  ( ad1 ad2 -- ad1' ad2' neg1 neg2 )
 ( a0 = address of $1           d0,d3 = temp    rp[ = temp addr ]
   a1 = address of $2           d1 = neg1
                                d2 = neg2 )
 sp )+ a0 move,  1 bsr,  d1 d2 move,  a0 rp -) move,
 sp )  a0 move,  1 bsr,
 a0 sp  ) move,  rp )+ sp -) move,              ( ad1 ad2 )
 d1 sp -) move,  d2 sp -) move,                 ( neg1 neg2 )
 next,
1 :l  a0 sp -) move,  i' sortmap a1 move,       ( a1: sort translation table )
 0 #n d0 moveq,  d0 d1 move,                    ( clear char and flag )
 begin,  a0 ) d0 .b move,                       ( not past limit )
   a1 d0 0 xw)d d3 .b move,  eq                 ( d3 = translated char )
 while,  1 #n a0 addq,  again,
 sp )+ a0 move,
 ascii + #n d0 .b cmpi,  2 eq bra,
 ascii - #n d0 .b cmpi,  eq
 if,  -1 #n d1 moveq,                           ( d1 = signflag )
   2 :l  tc' ^nextchar jsr,                     ( a0 = new address )
 then,  rts,  ;c

code @digit?  ( addr -- char flag
   a0 = addr                    d0 = char
   a1 = table addresses         d1 = temp )
 sp ) a0 move,  0 #n d0 moveq,  d0 d2 move,  i' sortmap a1 move,
 begin,  a0 ) d0 .b move,
   a1 d0 0 xw)d d2 .b move,  eq
 while,  1 #n a0 addq,  again,
 d2 sp ) move,  0 #n sp -) move,                        ( char and flag )
 t' #table +ttable a1 move,  2 #n a1 addq,
 i' #chars d1 move,  1 #n d1 subq,
 begin,  a1 )+ d0 .b cmp,  d1 eq -until,  1 eq bra,     ( char = digit? )
 i' commapun 3 + d0 .b cmp,  1 eq bra,                  ( char = commapun? )
 i' dpoint 3 + d0 .b cmp,  eq                           ( char = dpoint? )
 if,  1 :l  -1 #n sp ) move,  then,  next;

code comparenumbers  ( addr1 lim1 addr2 lim2 -- ad1 ad2 compareflag )

 ( a0 = temp                            d0 = temp
   a1 = addr of $1                      d1 = limit of $1
   a2 = addr of $2                      d2 = limit of $2
   a3 = temp                            d3 = exitflag
   a4 = sortmap                         d4 = char
   a5 = saved start addr $1             d5 = count1
   a6 = saved start addr $2             d6 = whole digits and count2
                                        d7 = limit addr )

 sp )+ d2 move,  sp )+ a0 move,
 sp )+ d1 move,  sp )+ a1 move,
 (regs a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 d4 d5 d6 d7 to) sp -) movem,
 i' sortmap a4 move,                            ( dedicated reg, for speed )
 0 #n d4 moveq,                                 ( clear char )
 i' commapun 3 + d4 .b move,                    ( setup constant for comma )
  a4 d4 0 xw)d atemp 1+ .b move,                ( atemp+1 = translated comma )
 i' dpoint 3 + d4 .b move,                      ( setup constant for decimal pt)
  a4 d4 0 xw)d atemp .b move,                   ( atemp = translated dpoint )
 a0 a2 move,  0b .w bsr,  d6 d5 move,           ( d5 = count2 - whole digits )
 a1 a0 move,  0b .w bsr,                        ( d6 = count1 )
 d5 d6 cmp,  ne                                 ( count1 <> count2? )
 if,  lt                                        ( count1 < count2? )
   if,  d6 d5 exg,  -1 #n d3 moveq,             ( yes.  d5 = count minimum )
   else,  1 #n d3 moveq,                        ( no.   d3 = exitflag )
   then,  d5 d6 sub,  0c .w bsr,                ( exit on non-zero leading )
 then,  0f .w bsr,                              ( compare common whole parts )
 begin,        ( next char is now either dpoint, non-numerical or past limit )
   -1 #n d3 moveq,  0a .w bsr,                  ( $2 = fraction? )
   1 #n d6 cmp,  lt                             ( -1=no dp, 0=no numerals )
   if,  1 #n d3 moveq,  0a .w bsr,
      d1 d7 move,
      1 #n d6 cmp,  07 lt .w bra,       ( no, end of subfields, exit 07 )
      a1 a0 move,  05 bra,                      ( $1: trailing 0's and dp's )
   then,  d6 d5 move,                           ( d5 = count2 of frac part )
   1 #n d3 moveq,  0a .w bsr,                   ( d6 = count1 of frac part )
   1 #n d6 cmp,  lt                             ( $1 = fraction? )
   if,  -1 #n d3 moveq,  a2 a0 move,
      d2 d7 move,  05 bra,                      ( $2: trailing 0's and dp's )
   then,                                        ( both have fractions )
   d5 d6 cmp,  lt                               ( count1 < count2? )
   if,  d6 d5 exg,  -1 #n d3 moveq,  then,      ( yes, switch flag & counts )
   d5 d6 sub,                                   ( d5 = min, d6 = remaining )
   0f .w bsr,                                   ( compare common fractions )
   d6 tst,  ne                                  ( either one longer? )
   if,  0c .w bsr,  then,               ( yes, exit on trailing non-zeroes )
 again,                                         ( move on to next fraction )

 begin,                                         ( exit, trailing zeros? )
   begin,  d3 tst,  pl                          ( d3 = exitflag )
      if,  a0 a1 move,                          ( a0 = nextchar's addr )
      else,  a0 a2 move,
      then,
05 :l  d7 a0 cmp,  07 gt bra,                   ( beyond limit?  yes, exit 07 )
      0d .w bsr,                                ( no, d4 = translated char )
      tc' ^nextchar jsr,                        ( prepare for next char )
      atemp d4 .b cmp,  ne                      ( char = dp? )
   until,  0e .w bsr,  eq                       ( no, char = digit? )
 while,  ascii 0 #n d4 .b cmpi,  08 ne bra,     ( digit <> 0, exit 08 )
 again,                                 ( end of keyfield or end of # subfield )

07 :l  0 #n d3 moveq,                           ( exit 07, 0 means $1 = $2 )

08 :l                                           ( exit 08, flag already set )
 a2 a0 move,                                    ( protect a2 )
 (regs a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 d4 d5 d6 d7 from) sp )+ movem,
 a1 sp -) move,  a0 sp -) move,  d3 sp -) move,
 next,                                          ( return to calling word )

09 :l                                           ( nextchar, not a comma )
 begin,  begin,  a0 ) d4 .b move,               ( d4 = untranslated char )
      tc' ^nextchar jsr,                        ( skip char - comma or not )
      a4 d4 0 xw)d d4 .b move,  ne              ( d4 = translated char )
   until,  atemp 1+ d4 .b cmp,  ne              ( char = comma? )
 until,  rts,                                   ( a0 = new address )

0a :l                                           ( count fraction part )
 d3 tst,  pl
 if,  a1 a0 move,  else,  a2 a0 move,  then,
 09 .w bsr,
 atemp d4 .b cmp,  ne                           ( first char = dp? )
 if,  -1 #n d6 moveq,  rts,  then,              ( no, set fractionflag )
 d3 tst,  pl                                    ( yes, continue count )
 if,  a0 a1 move,  else,  a0 a2 move,  then,    ( skip dp for later compare )

0b :l                                           ( count whole digits )
 0 #n d6 moveq,                                 ( clear count )
 begin,  09 .w bsr,  0e .w bsr,  eq             ( skip commas, d4 = char )
 while,  1 #n d6 addq,                          ( d6 = count of whole digits )
 again,  rts,

0c :l                                           ( exit on leading zero )
 d3 tst,  pl
 if,  a1 a0 move,  else,  a2 a0 move,  then,
 1 #n d6 subq,                                  ( d3 = exitflag, d6 = count )
 begin,  0d .w bsr,                             ( d4 = char, skip commas )
   ascii 0 #n d4 .b cmpi,  ne                   ( char = zero? )
   if,  sp )+ a3 move,                          ( no, drop return address )
      08 .w bra,                                ( exit )
   then,  tc' ^nextchar jsr,                    ( ignore zeros )
 d6 nt -until,  d3 tst,  pl
 if,  a0 a1 move,  else,  a0 a2 move,  then,    ( adjust addr pointer )
 rts,

0d :l                                   ( returns d4 = thischar, not commas )
 begin,  begin,  a0 ) d4 .b move,               ( accents nor attributes )
      a4 d4 0 xw)d d4 .b move,  eq              ( a4 = sortmap )
   while,  1 #n a0 addq,  again,                ( attr or accent, get next )
   atemp 1+ d4 .b cmp,  eq                      ( char = comma? )
 while,  tc' ^nextchar jsr,                     ( skip only commas )
 again,  rts,

0e :l                                           ( char = digit? )
 t' #table +ttable a3 move,  2 #n a3 addq,
 i' #chars d0 move,  1 #n d0 subq,
 begin,  a3 )+ d4 .b cmp,  d0 eq -until,  rts,

0f :l  d5 tst,  ne
 if,  1 #n d5 subq,
   begin,                                       ( whole digit compare )
      a1 a0 move,  09 .w bsr,  a0 a1 move,      ( skip commas in $1 )
      d4 d7 move,
      a2 a0 move,  09 .w bsr,  a0 a2 move,      ( skip commas in $2 )
      d7 d4 cmp,  ne                            ( digits don't match, exit )
      if,  gt                                   ( digits not equal )
         if,  -1 #n d3 moveq,           ( d3 = exitflag, -1 means $1 < $2 )
         else,  1 #n d3 moveq,                  ( and 1 means $1 > $2 )
         then,  sp )+ d7 move,  08 .w bra,      ( pop return addr, exit 08 )
      then,
   d5 nt -until,
 then,  rts,

;c

: $<  ( addr1 lim1 addr2 lim2 -- compareflag )
      ( -1 = $1<$2, 0 = equal, 1 = $1>$2 )
 local ad1     local ad2                        ( addresses of strings )
 local len1    local len2                       ( lengths of strings )
 local limit1  local limit2                     ( string limit addr )
 local len                      ( flag: compared length of numerical subfields )
 local neg                              ( sign of strings if they're the same )
 len2 to ad2 to  len1 to ad1 to
 len1 0= len2 0= or                             ( check for null string cases )
 if  len2 0 <>  exit  then                      ( -1 or 0 )
 ( ad1 ad2 =                                    ( addresses match!! )
 ( if  len1 len2 <>  if  len1 len2 < 2* 1+      ( compare lengths )
 ( else  0  then  exit  then            ( done! -- actually takes longer! )
 ad1 len1 + limit1 to
 ad2 len2 + limit2 to                           ( limits are now set )
 len off  neg off                               ( clear length & sign flags )
 ad1 limit1  ad2 limit2  signed?                ( are both signed numbers? )
 if  ad1 ad2 checksigns                         ( examine signs )
   over neg to  <>                              ( sign of both if same )
   if  2drop  neg 2* 1+  exit  then             ( different signs, done )
   limit1 swap limit2  comparenumbers  ?dup     ( compare numerically )
   if  nip nip  neg 2* 1+ *  exit  then         ( neg: reverse flag )
   over ad1 -  over ad2 -  2dup >               ( calc lengths )
   if  2drop  1  else  <  then  len to          ( len = -1, 0 or 1 )
   ad2 to  ad1 to
 then
 begin  ad1 @digit? nip
   ad2 @digit? nip and                          ( both are numbers? )
   if  ad1 limit1 ad2 limit2
      comparenumbers  ?dup                      ( compare numerically )
      if  nip nip  exit  then                   ( different values, done )
      len 0=                                    ( need to compare lengths? )
      if  over ad1 -  over ad2 -  2dup >        ( calc lengths )
         if  2drop  1  else  <  then  len to    ( len = -1, 0 or 1 )
      then  ad2 to  ad1 to                      ( update addresses )
   else  ad1 limit1 ad2 limit2
      comparestrings  ?dup                      ( compare strings )
      if  nip nip  exit  then                   ( different strings, done )
      ad2 to  ad1 to
   then  ad1 limit1 < ad2 limit2 < and not      ( until one string runs out )
 until  ad1 limit1 <                            ( still have some left in $1? )
 if  1                                          ( yes, $2 < $1 )
 else  ad2 limit2 <                             ( no, have some left in $2? )
   if  -1                                       ( yes, $1 < $2 )
   else  len
      if  len                                   ( one w/ shortest = # subfield )
      else  len1 len2 <>
         if  len1 len2 < 2* 1+                  ( one that is shortest overall )
         else  0                                ( both are the same length )
 then  then  then  then ;

code builduplinks  ( -- \ fill in links in backward chain )
 i' sorttop #n a0 move,  ulink #n d0 moveq,
 begin,  a0 ) a1 move,  0 #n a1 cmp,  ne
 while,  a0 a1 d0 0 xw)d move,  a1 a0 move,
 again,  a0 i' sortbot move,  next;

code nextfield ( addr -- addr' )
 sp )+ a0 move,  0 #n d0 moveq,         ( starting address )
 begin,  a0 )+ d0 .b move,              ( scanning forward for a tab )
   rtn #n d0 .b cmp,  ls
   if,   tb #n d0 .b cmp,  nc
      if,  1 #n a0 subq,  a0 sp -) move,  next,  then,
   then,  &skip #n d0 .b cmp,  eq  if,  tc' ^sk> jsr,  then,
 again,  ;c

: prevpkt?  ( addr -- addr\addr' = addr of packet if prevchar = break w/pkt )
 dup prevchar fpkt?  if  prevchar brk+ then ;

: nextrecord   ( addr -- addr' )
   local done
   begin  done on  nextbrk  sortbreaks 1-
      if  sortbreaks 1-  0
         do  nextchar dup c@  break? not
            if  done off  leave  then
   loop  then  done  until ;

: newnode   ( maxsize pointer -- pointer' )
  local pointer  pointer to
  pointer  if  bou pointer -  else  0  then
  rsize + ( maxsize ) + 5 +  needtext  0=
  if  noroom error  abort  then
  pointer
  if  pointer rsize - dup pointer olink + !  dup pointer !
  else  bou dup 1 and - rsize -  dup eosl to
  then  dup rsize 0 fill ;

: buildlist ( -- head-of-list )
   local maxsize  local pointer  local end  local oldtop
   maxsize off    pointer off    top oldtop to
   gap prevpkt? end to  clearundo  preset
   bos dup pop to
   begin  dup nextrecord dup gap <              ( find end of record )
   while  maxsize pointer newnode  pointer to
      top oldtop <>                             ( forth boundary moved? )
      if  2drop  bos dup nextrecord             ( yes, start over )
         maxsize 0 newnode  pointer to  top oldtop to  then
      begin  nextchar dup c@ break? not  until  ( until new line )
      gap min                                   ( make sure in selection )
      prevpkt?                                  ( include prev format packet )
      over pointer recaddr + !                  ( set address of record )
      2dup swap - dup pointer reclen + w!       ( & length of record )
      maxsize over <  if  maxsize to  else  drop  then  ( set maxs )
      over tab#
      if  tab# 0  do  nextfield nextchar  loop
         2dup <  if  drop dup  then             ( if not, use end )
      else  prevchar nextchar  then             ( skip format packets )
      rot over swap - pointer foffset + w!      ( set field start )
      swap over nextfield 2dup <                ( check field len )
      if  drop dup  then  rot -                 ( next tab or end )
      pointer flen + w!                         ( set field length )
   again  2drop  pointer bosl to  eosl sorttop to
   0 sparepkt !                         ( so postshuffle won't insert sparepkt )
   builduplinks ;                               ( fill in upward links )

code 2swap  ( a b c d -- c d a b )
 sp )+ d0 move,  ( d )  sp )+ d1 move,  ( c )
 sp 4 )d sp -) move,  ( a )  sp 4 )d sp -) move,  ( b )
 d1 sp c )d move,  ( c )  d0 sp 8 )d move,  ( d )  next;

: getstring  ( recordpointer -- addr len )
 dup recaddr + @ over foffset + w@ +    ( string address )
 swap flen + w@ ;                       ( length)

code countlist  ( bosl eosl -- count )
 sp )+ a0 move,  sp ) a1 move,  1 #n d0 moveq,          ( a0: eosl, a1: bosl )
 a0 a1 cmp,  ne
 if,                                                    ( bosl <> eosl )
   begin,  0 #n a0 cmp,  ne
   while,  1 #n d0 addq,  a0 ) a0 move,  a0 a1 cmp,  eq ( count # elements )
   until,  then,  d0 sp ) move,  next;

: scansublist  ( focal bosl eosl flag -- pointer \ true means look for greater )
               ( pointer=0 means in order, else pointer is out of order )
 local addr    local len
 local flag    flag to
 local low     swap low to
 2dup =  if  2drop 0  then                      ( bad sublist )
 swap getstring len to addr to
 begin  dup getstring addr len                  ( $1=pointer, $2=focal )
   flag  if  2swap  then                        ( true = $2=pointer, $1=focal )
   $< -1 <>                                     ( -1 means $1 < $2 )
 while  @  dup low =                            ( gotten all the way through? )
   if  drop 0  leave  then  dup 0=              ( yes, return 0 )
 until  ?panic
 if  notsorted error collapse abort  then ;     ( allow interruptions )

code prevrec  ( record -- record+ )
 sp ) a0 move,  0 #n a0 cmp,  ne
 if,  a0 ulink )d sp ) move,
 else,  i' sortbot sp ) move,  then,  next;

code insertrec  ( rec ptr lowbound -- rec- \ insert rec before ptr )
 sp )+ d2 move,  ulink #n d3 moveq,     ( d2: lowbound, d3: up link offset )
 sp )+ d1 move,  sp ) a0 move,          ( a0: rec, d1: ptr )
 a0 ) d0 move,  a0 d3 0 xw)d a1 move,   ( d0: rec-, a1: rec+ )
 d0 a1 ) move,  d0 sp ) move,           ( point rec+ to rec-, return rec- )
 d0 d2 cmp,  eq  if,  0 #n sp ) move,  then,    ( if rec- = lowbound, return 0 )
 d0 tst,  ne                            ( rec was bottom of list? )
 if,  a1 d2 move,  d0 a1 move,          ( no, d2: rec+ )
   d2 a1 d3 0 xw)d move,                ( up point rec- to rec+ )
 else,  a1 i' sortbot move,             ( yes, set new bottom of list )
 then,  d1 a0 ) move,  d1 tst,  eq      ( point rec to ptr, rec = list bottom? )
 if,  i' sortbot a1 move,               ( yes, get bottom of list )
   a0 i' sortbot move,                  ( set new bottom of list )
 else,  d1 a1 move,                     ( no, a1: ptr )
   a1 d3 0 xw)d d0 move,                ( d0: ptr+ )
   a0 a1 d3 0 xw)d move,  d0 a1 move,   ( up point ptr to rec )
 then,  a0 a1 ) move,                   ( point ptr+ to rec )
 a1 a0 d3 0 xw)d move,  next;           ( up point rec to ptr+ )

: selectionsort  ( bottom@ top -- bottom@ )
 upp to lpl to
 begin  lpl upp countlist 2 >
 while
   begin  upp lpl over @ descending scansublist  ?dup
   while  upp over upp to lpl  insertrec  drop
   again  upp @ dup upp to  0=
 until  lpl ;

: quicksort  ( bottom@ top -- bottom@ )
 rdepth largestacksize 2 shr >
 if  notsorted error  collapse  abort  then             ( too much recursion )
 2dup =  if  drop exit  then                            ( nothing to sort ... )
 2dup  countlist  dup 0b <                              ( 0b = empirical # )
 if  drop  selectionsort  exit  then                    ( quicksort is slower )
 over upp to      upp prevrec hpl to                    ( always points to top )
 2+ 2/ 2+ 2/ 0 do @ loop  focal to                      ( make 2 sublists )
 focal @ lpp to   lpl to                                ( bottom@ )
 begin  focal dup upp descending 0= scansublist  dup upp to     ( clean upper )
 while  upp lpp focal insertrec  dup upp to 0=  until           ( sublist )
 begin  focal lpl lpp descending scansublist  dup lpp to        ( clean lower )
 while  lpp focal lpl insertrec  dup lpp to 0=  until           ( sublist )
 lpl focal @ ulink + !                                  ( preserve lpl )
 focal hpl @ quicksort focal to                         ( sort upper sublist )
 focal dup @ ulink +  dup @ lpl to  !                   ( restore lpl, uplink )
 lpl focal @ quicksort ;                                ( « return stack usage )

code moverecords  ( source destination length list -- )
 ( move block & adjust list for all records in the block )
 sp )+ a0 move,  sp ) d2 move,          ( a0: list, d2: length )
 sp 4 )d d1 move,  sp 8 )d d0 move,     ( d0: source, d1: dest )
 d0 d1 sub,  d0 d2 add,                 ( d1: delta, d2: end+1 )
 begin,  i' recaddr d3 move,  a0 d3 0 xw)d d3 move,
   d2 d3 cmp,  lt                       ( record < end+1? )
   if,  d0 d3 cmp,  ge                  ( record > source? )
      if,  d1 d3 add,  d3 rp -) move,   ( inrange )
         i' recaddr d3 move,  rp )+ a0 d3 0 xw)d move,  then,  then,
   a0 ) a0 move,  0 #n a0 cmp,  eq
 until,  tc' move jmp,  ;c

code largestrec  ( listhead pointer -- pointer' )
 sp )+ a0 move,  0 #n a0 cmp,  ne                       ( a0: pointer )
 if,  i' recaddr d0 move,  a0 d0 0 xw)d d3 move,        ( d3: maximum record )
 else,  i' gap d3 move,  then,
 sp ) a0 move,                                          ( a0: list )
 0 #n d1 moveq,  d1 d2 move,                    ( d1: pointer, d2: largest rec )
 begin,   i' recaddr d0 move,  a0 d0 0 xw)d d0 move,    ( d0: record )
   d3 d0 cmp, lt
   if,  d2 d0 cmp,  gt
      if,  d0 d2 move,  a0 d1 move,  then,  then,       ( largestrec, pointer' )
   a0 ) a0 move,  0 #n a0 cmp,  eq
 until,  d1 sp ) move,  next,  ;c

code moverecord  ( flag dest list -- dest' list' \ -1=below, 0=above )
 sp )+ a0 move,  sp )+ d1 move,                 ( a0: list, d1: destination )
 0 #n d0 moveq,  i' reclen d3 move,
 a0 d3 0 xw)d d0 .w move,                       ( w@, d0: len )
 0 #n sp ) cmp,  mi                             ( return new dest: )
 if,  d0 d1 sub,  d1 sp ) move,                 ( mi: move below )
 else,  d0 sp ) move,  d1 sp ) add,  then,      ( pl: move above )
 a0 ) sp -) move,                               ( return new list pointer )
 i' recaddr d3 move,  a0 d3 0 xw)d sp -) move,  ( fetch old record addr )
 d1 a0 d3 0 xw)d move,                          ( store new record addr )
 d1 sp -) move,  d0 sp -) move,  tc' move jmp,  ;c

: moveunsorted  ( list -- )
 local pointer    local list    local top
 list to          gap top to    pointer off
 begin  list pointer largestrec dup pointer to
 while  -1 top pointer moverecord drop top to  again ;

: adjustsortlist  ( delta listtop -- )
 local list    list to
 local delta   delta to
 delta bos +to  delta gap +to
 list
 begin  dup recaddr + dup @ delta +
   dup bos <  over bos =  0 delta <  and  or
   if  2drop  dup reclen + dup w@ delta + swap w!
   else  swap !  then
   @ dup 0=
 until  drop ;

: preshuffle  ( listtop -- )
 local listtop  listtop to
 bos prevchar pop to                            ( prevchar won't move )
 showmove? off                                  ( move&adjtext won't display )
 gap prevchar fpkt?
 if  pktsize negate gap +to
   gap workpkt pktsize  move                    ( stash trailing format pkt )
 else  0 workpkt !
 then  sparepkt @ 0=
 if  bos prevchar fpkt?
   if  bos prevpkt? sparepkt pktsize move       ( save leading pkt for undo )
      bos dup prevpkt? gap bos - move           ( remove from text )
      pktsize negate listtop adjustsortlist     ( update list accordingly )
 then  then ;

: postshuffle  ( listtop pop' -- )
 local listtop  pop to  listtop to
 workpkt @
 if  workpkt gap pktsize move  pktsize gap +to  ( restore trailing format pkt? )
 then  0 workpkt !
 pop prevchar fpkt? 0=                          ( pop follows pkt? )
 if  sparepkt @                                 ( no, original pkt saved? )
   if  pop dup pktsize + gap pop - move         ( yes, make room for orig pkt )
      sparepkt pop pktsize move                 ( replace original pkt )
      pktsize listtop adjustsortlist            ( adjust list for undo )
      pop nextchar pop to
      0 sparepkt !
 then  then  pop prevchar gap killivls beot partknown
 gap prevchar bos to  beot eos to  rewindow
 showmove? on                                   ( move&adjtext will display )
 ['] undosort undop to dirtytext? on ;

: shuffle ( sortedlist -- )
 local list          list to                    ( the sorted list )
 local oldlist       list oldlist to            ( the unsorted list )
 list preshuffle
 local unsorted      bos prevpkt? unsorted to   ( nothing unsorted yet )
 local sorted        gap sorted to              ( position to move records to )
 begin  list reclen + w@  bosl sorted -  >      ( room for record? )
   if  list moveunsorted                        ( no, reorganize )
      gap unsorted sorted gap - oldlist moverecords     ( move sorted records )
      sorted gap - unsorted +to
      gap sorted to
   then  0 sorted list moverecord list to sorted to
   list 0=
 until  sorted gap <>                           ( no more to shuffle )
 if  gap unsorted sorted gap - oldlist  moverecords     ( move sorted records )
 then  oldlist pop nextchar postshuffle ;


: prevrecsep  ( addr -- addr' \ of previous record separator )
   local done                                   ( flag: false = not record sep )
   begin  done on  prevbrk
      sortbreaks 1- ?dup
      if  0  do  prevchar dup c@  break? not
         if  done off  leave  then  loop  then
   done until ;

: findfield ( addr -- field# \ field# = -1 means not enough records to sort )
   local pos  pos to                            ( the address in the field )
   local field#   field# off                    ( assume no field )
   local record
   pos prevrecsep dup
   sortbreaks 0  do  nextchar  loop  record to  ( now just after record sep )
   record pos prevchar nextchar < not
   if  prevrecsep  then
   bos bot max > not  if  -1  exit  then        ( not enough records to sort )
   record
   begin  nextfield dup pos <                   ( count fields until to pos )
   while  1 field# +to 1+
   again  drop  field# ;                        ( return field number )

: adjustleadingbrks  ( -- )
   gap prevchar  bos
   begin  2dup >
   while  dup c@ break?                         ( skip any leading breaks )
   while  nextchar
   again  bos to  drop ;                        ( reset bos to here )

: adjustrailingbrks  ( -- flag )
   beot
   begin  prevchar dup c@ break? 0=             ( strip trailing breaks )
   until  sortbreaks 1 =                        ( include rest of record )
   if  nextbrk                                  ( sortbreak = 1 )
   else  begin  nextbrk  sortbreaks 1- 0        ( sortbreak > 1 )
         do  nextchar dup c@ break? 0=          ( not enough breaks? )
            if  dup eor <
               if  0 leave
               else  drop ( addr )  0 exit  then
            then  loop  ?dup                    ( past local region? )
         if  leave  then
      again
   then  nextchar  dup eor <
   if  eos to movegap  -1                       ( include end of record )
   else  drop  0  then ;                ( not enough breaks to make a record )

: adjustformats  ( -- )
   local pktmark  pktmark off                   ( preserve trailing format )
   gap prevchar fpkt? 0=
   if  gap
      begin prevbrk dup nextchar 1+ bos >       ( examine every selected break )
      while  dup fpkt?                          ( it has a format packet? )
         if  pktmark                            ( yes, has more than one? )
            if  pktmark gap pktsize move        ( yes, copy last packet )
               pktsize gap +to  leave           ( to end and stop looking )
            else  dup brk+ pktmark to  then  then       ( no, point to packet )
   again  drop  then ;

: presort  ( -- flag \ prepare selection for sorting )
   ( preceding breaks are left alone by sorting 
   ( the number of fields in the last record becomes the sort field,
   ( except that if the cursor follows at least sortbreaks breaks it is 0, and
   ( only sortbreaks number of trailing breaks are included
   ( in the sort selection )
   showmove? off                                ( move&adjtext won't display )
   bos pop to  undop off
   selected  lockedsel                          ( see if selection is locked )
   bos nextchar gap >  if  0 exit  then
   adjustleadingbrks
   gap findfield dup tab# to                    ( establish sorting column )
   0<  if  0  exit  then                        ( nothing to sort )
   adjustrailingbrks  dup
   if  drop  adjustformats  -1 then ;
code swaplinks  ( list -- list )
 sp ) d0 move,  ne                                      ( d0: top of list )
 if,  i' olink d1 move,                                 ( d1: olink )
   begin,  d0 a0 move,  a0 d1 0 xw)d d0 move,           ( a0: pointer )
      a0 ) a0 d1 0 xw)d move,  d0 a0 ) move,            ( d0: new pointer )
      d0 tst,  eq
 until,  then,  next;

: redosort ( -- )
 indsort rule                                   ( indicate sorting )
 0 sparepkt !
 undop off  pop bos to
 sorttop swaplinks shuffle
 display  widecursor  forceop on  fixcursor
 " " 3 indicate  rule ;

: undosort ( -- )
 indsort rule                                   ( indicate sorting )
 undop off  pop bos to
 eosl swaplinks shuffle
 pop op to  extend
 ['] redosort undop to  fixcursor
 " " 3 indicate  rule ;

: sort  ( -- )
 indsort rule                                   ( indicate sorting )
 presort
 if  buildlist
   0 sorttop quicksort drop     ( sort the list )
   sorttop shuffle              ( arrange the records according to the list )
   pop op to  display
 else  nosort error
   gap prevchar dup bos <>
   if  bos op to  then  bos to  redisplay
 then  widecursor  forceop on  fixcursor
 " " 3 indicate  rule ;

: aSort  ( -- | ascending sort )  descending off  sort ;

: dSort  ( -- | descending sort )  descending on  sort ;

( debugging words for Sort )

: sitem  ( addr -- )
 cr dup 6 .r
   dup @ 7 .r
   dup ulink + @ 7 .r
   dup olink + @ 7 .r
   dup recaddr + @ 7 .r
   dup reclen + w@ 4 .r
   dup foffset + w@ 4 .r
   dup flen + w@ 5 .r
   dup recaddr + @ pad 10 move
   pad 10 afilter  3 spaces  pad 10 type ;

: .slist  ( addr which -- \ display contents of sort list, for debugging )
 local which  which to
 cr ."   list  slink  ulink  olink record len off flen   10 bytes"
 begin  sitem  which + @ dup 0= ?keystep or  until  drop ;

: slist  ( -- \ display contents of sort list, for debugging )
 sorttop 0 .slist ;

: olist  ( -- \ display contents of sort list, for debugging )
 eosl olink .slist ;

: unlist  ( -- \ display contents of sort list, for debugging )
 eosl 0 .slist ;
